## docs/DARWIN-CONNECT-PREFIX-PROTECTION-EVIDENCE-2026-11-16.md

# macOS protection for complete CONNECT execution paths

The existing macOS ordered firewall helper now accepts the complete three-to-eight-member
CONNECT execution shape already admitted by the desktop controller. It retains every
paired prefix row, the penultimate HTTP or HTTPS CONNECT member, the paired Shadowsocks
final, and the strongest captured protection and LAN policy. SOCKS execution keeps its
separate three-member bound. This changes no endpoint exception or native source authority.

The original saved-profile reconstruction, member digest, private journal, PF reference,
retirement, and receipt checks remain in the shipping call path. Local CONNECT and
CONNECT execution can now both describe a longer path; their receipts and cleanup
authority remain distinct. A valid shape alone cannot adopt or release the other owner.

## Executed validation

On source base `0b42100a1246aed3a1c08e6cca662abfccb5e305` plus this change,
all 114 tests in `modes/test_darwin_owned_pf.py` passed in 8.504 seconds. The
existing imported-execution coverage now exercises four- and eight-node CONNECT
captures as well as the original three-node captures, with WG, AWG Fast and AWG Strong.
Saved-member replacement cases still require rejection before a native mutation.
A new test checks current/readback, retirement and release with the other legal
CONNECT family's capture, with and without the original receipt, and verifies
that the actual journal and command list remain unchanged.

The complete `deploy/test_darwin_pf_journal.py` gate also passed, including all
original isolated source-defect checks and the added defect that reinstates
three-node-only CONNECT execution. That defect must fail the original saved-member
baseline assertion. The SOCKS length and Shadowsocks admission defects retain
their separate controls. Existing per-process time limits are unchanged.

These tests execute the shipping Python dispatcher, private filesystem and journal
with the suite's explicit PF/kernel model. They do not establish installed macOS
packet behavior, encrypted networking, device acceptance, or a green release.
The full gate remains required by the existing macOS workflow.

The concurrent desktop CONNECT implementation was preserved. Four overlapping
unpublished Go draft edits were retained in a recovery patch and were not replayed
over that upstream commit; this unit closes the independently observed firewall
helper gap.

## docs/DARWIN-OFF-FIRST-FAILURE-EVIDENCE-2026-11-16.md

# Darwin Off native fixture: preserve the first failed family

## Observed failure

The retained log for macOS physical socket run `43768501865`, job `132978897101`, at historical source `c7f43af`, shows the deliberately broken “Off preserves ordinary host traffic” control reaching its intended assertion in TCP4, UDP4 and TCP6. The fixture nevertheless started UDP6 after those failures. That last ordinary `net.Conn.Write` reported 33,554,436 bytes for a 38-byte input, followed by the existing poll-layer panic and race reports from the oversized read range. The native runner correctly rejected that transcript; it was never a passing control.

The upstream [Go issue 61060](https://github.com/golang/go/issues/61060) records the same 33,554,436 return count in Darwin networking and packet-filter scenarios. This is supporting context, not a claim to have reproduced or repaired the kernel/runtime cause here. No production networking, Go runtime, race instrumentation or packet-filter policy is changed.

## Fixture change

`TestDesktopDarwinGuardianKernelOffHostAndBroker` now returns when a synchronous `t.Run` reports failure. The child’s registered cleanup finishes before that return. The first actual failed boundary remains a failure, and another family does not begin after it. Successful execution still runs TCP4, UDP4, TCP6 and UDP6 with the original source, assertions, ownership checks, deadlines and cleanup. The unchanged positive validator requires all four unique passing child verdicts and their passing parent.

The mutation validator additionally rejects the actual cleanup message `original Darwin Off guardian did not retire`, even when the transcript also contains the intended ordinary-host denial. Existing panic, race, timeout, skip and cleanup rejection remains intact.

## Executed evidence

A Go race executable compiles the actual family loop extracted from the committed native fixture. Explicit helper boundaries model only sequencing and cleanup; they do not claim PF or socket acceptance. It verifies the complete successful inventory, a failure in each of the four original positions, a cleanup-only failure, and an intended assertion followed by failed cleanup. Every entered family completes its registered cleanup, and no later family enters after failure. Restoring the original continue-after-failure loop fails that dispatch requirement. All eight observations completed under the original small regression process bounds.

The focused compiled-loop check passed in 2.166 seconds. The five existing mutation-overlay checks and the existing SOCKS/Off native-verdict tests passed. The preserved outputs are `darwin-off-family-sequence.log` and `darwin-off-family-verdicts.log` in this continuation workspace.

Actual Intel and Apple Silicon PF/guardian execution remains the mandatory hosted gate. This publication establishes the fixture sequencing and strict verdict correction, not a native kernel pass, a new release, or full product completion.


---

## docs/DARWIN-PAIRED-SS-EXECUTION-2026-11-16.md

# Darwin paired Shadowsocks execution — 2026-11-16

The desktop parent and AppKit editor admit a distinct, wholly paired WG/AWG prefix ending in Shadowsocks2022 for Server and Compare on macOS. Complete sources contain **2–8 members**. The generic Darwin capability remains closed; imported, typed and Hysteria families retain their separate admission. Actual backend preparation revalidates the original complete source, independent credentials, identities, selected DNS, protection, MTU and retained physical owner. The existing generic child, Ready, Last and PF retirement paths remain authoritative.

The new required installed-host job covers **14 direct two-member cases on both Darwin architectures**, using captured Off layers: WG and both AWG modes; Server and both Compare winners; temporary-session restoration; wrong public/final identity, selected DNS failure, changed private source, and a wrong final encryption key. It consumes the exact current native bundle, actual controller/child/PF/utun paths, encrypted peers and the existing portable direct Server worker. Its fixture-owned aliases cannot retire before the original peer and worker join. These installed cases have **not run locally**.

Longer Server suffixes retain the actual **Linux-only packet carrier**. Four/eight-member source and ownership cases do not establish same-host Darwin Server packet carriers or cross-host encrypted traffic. Independently supported Start Layers remain admitted and source-tested; the new direct native fixture does not claim a complete layer combination matrix.

## Local validation

| Boundary | Result and limit |
| --- | --- |
| Original source baseline on `9d5307d0` plus the draft | Full `cmd/client` race compilation and vet passed in 172.60s; three actual source/ownership roots passed 21 records. This is not an exact-final-main release run. |
| Compiled causal controls | Duplicate-proof and illegal-layer omissions reached their intended assertions. The separately corrected retained-physical recapture omission compiled in 169.04s and reached its sole intended refusal witness. |
| Shipping Swift callbacks | Full positive program passed 35,431 checks, plus three intact selected compiled controls, in 49.16s. All 81 hosted controls remain required. Linux Swift execution is not native AppKit rendering. |
| Alias ownership | Actual extracted shipping helper/lock passed two roots and 14 leaves plus six compiled controls; OS observations are explicit adapters, not native alias acceptance. |
| Darwin ARM64 package | Full privileged-tag `cmd/client` cross-compilation and vet passed in 115.77s; no Darwin test execution or race claim. |
| Registered native peer | Full race compilation passed in 80.07s over a 1,637-file current-source overlay of the unchanged pinned core. The existing concrete authentication-error witness passed. No provider traffic is inferred from compilation or that witness. |
| Required-gate checks | 36 Python runner roots, four impacted source-audit roots, and the independent full artifact/workflow parity audit passed. |

All original runtime, assertion and cleanup deadlines remain unchanged: parent compile 240s; source/control test 120s and process 135s; Swift child 90s; peer compile 300s. The successful corrected control used two CPUs, GOGC 25 and 600MiB, separately recorded from the earlier 900MiB/GOGC 50 attempts.

Failures remain in the evidence: the first third-control mutation did not compile; its next version targeted an unexercised projection guard and therefore earned no causal credit; the corrected reader mutation initially encountered a killed compiler; a later attempt stopped at disk preflight before compilation. The first native peer build exhausted disk during dependency compilation, before producing an image or running tests. None is counted as a successful gate. The source baseline, current cross-compilation and per-control snapshots have separate identities. Current composition preserves the published Hysteria layer changes, OpenVPN native gate, audit repair and SOCKS source inventory. The final upstream Hysteria physical-factory change is preserved separately: its existing Shadowsocks branch is unchanged; the retained peer compilation predates that unrelated Hysteria edit and is not an exact-final-tree release claim.

## Evidence identities

These SHA-256 identities identify the retained execution records; scratch paths are not repository-persisted artifacts. The final evidence index digest is `6692dd8c91bc85e900738ce4e8d2674909de9c7c1233adf8aa66774676584115`.

## docs/DESKTOP-CANDIDATE-COMPLETE-PROPOSAL-2026-11-16.md

# Complete desktop next-hop proposals

Date: 2026-11-16. This is a bounded source and controller change for the
reconciled external-node, routed-candidate measurement and complete connection
profile requirements. It is not a whole-project completion statement.

## Problem and resulting behavior

The old desktop proposal builder copied only the measured prefix, appended the
candidate, and sometimes reattached the final node. That could discard other
saved members. It also replaced the requested execution with Local.

The proposal now copies the complete original two-to-eight-node selection and
replaces exactly the next slot after the measured prefix. Every other member
and the retained final control profile remain unchanged. An imported transport
must fit its supported position in that complete graph. An incompatible
candidate cannot acquire Save by shortening the original path.

Connected candidate catalogs and Save read the execution from the original
bound execution preparation. This preserves Server or Compare even when the
saved profile still says Local. Temporary saved-path preparation retains its
captured choice. A foreign execution owner cannot provide that choice.

The complete proposal runs the actual generic, SOCKS or CONNECT source compiler.
Server and Compare proposals also run their actual execution-preparation
compiler. The corresponding OpenVPN branch retains its distinct compiler.
These calls compile and validate sources; saving a proposal does not start a
tunnel or grant a Connected or authenticated-node proof.

Saving keeps reference-only private-node associations. Explicit Load applies
the complete profile transactionally. Source, policy, execution, ownership and
fresh-result checks remain required. This change does not alter the 50-sample
TCP measurement's meaning or turn it into HTTP, ICMP or credential proof.

## Relevant source

- `cmd/client/ordered_candidates.go`: complete next-slot replacement and bound
  execution selection for the connected catalog.
- `cmd/client/ordered_candidates_profile.go`: complete family and execution
  compilation before source-bound Save.
- `cmd/client/ordered_candidate_complete_proposal_test.go`: complete Local,
  Server and Compare proposals, original-source retention and refusal cases.
- `cmd/client/ordered_socks_candidates_profile_test.go`: the paired positive
  fixture replaces the actual final slot; it no longer depends on truncation.

## Validation at publication — runtime checks still pending

The retained driver compiles the whole canonical `cmd/client` package with its
pinned Go toolchain, race instrumentation, ordinary optimization and vet. It
requires 19 complete test roots and 155 exact test records: 35 existing closed
prefix receipt/cleanup records, 120 complete-proposal/source/Save records, and
three separately compiled legacy-proposal rejection controls.

The new complete-proposal matrix covers three, four and eight nodes with
imported Shadowsocks 2022, SOCKS5, HTTP CONNECT and HTTPS CONNECT, under Local,
Server and Compare. It checks the complete node list, retained independent
source identities, final policy, real family and execution compilers, saved
record, append/rollback and the public Load endpoint. Separate negatives cover
unsupported placement, duplicate identities, invalid execution, replaced
private sources and foreign captured execution ownership.

The existing native-receipt and physical-observation doubles remain explicit.
These controller checks do not execute a new encrypted transport, installed
desktop package, physical device or private production server. Exact-SHA native
builds/release and live acceptance remain separate evidence.

The first local positive run exposed a fixture mismatch: Load normalizes legacy
empty Base/DNS fields. The fixture now starts from explicit saved preferences;
strict whole-profile retention assertions remain intact. A subsequent full
compiler was killed before producing a test image. The final local retry used
one Go worker, `GOGC=25` and a 700 MiB Go memory target. It reached the original
240-second compiler timeout without an image or executed test roots. The
shipping compilation, race instrumentation, vet, 120-second test-root limits
and all negative controls remain unchanged. No successful runtime verdict is
claimed for the corrected fixtures at publication; the full hosted suite must
verify this exact committed source.

The initial full race compilation completed in 136.592 seconds on the recorded
`5fce4d2c` composition. Its production bodies match this change; its Local root
failed the explicit preference-fixture check described above. The final
publication composes the unchanged four owned paths on `4ae6a3c4`, preserving
the concurrent Hysteria2 final-layer implementation. The initial, killed and
timeout attempt receipts are retained with SHA-256 identities respectively:

```text
5f6b88367b2957f0ca5a15ead336400cbf36107fafa5d36bf3b6ffdc4fdb1948
557ec525137e8e2d0aaa275932ea1a11ccc37edaa4d4405ee85b01ddaeeb28a6
4558e0bb7776f8db4aa88426791ad8f5359dbafd4bd33e1231c2236b6da2fd93
```

## docs/DESKTOP-CANDIDATE-PROPOSAL-SOURCE-EVIDENCE-2026-11-16.md

# Complete candidate proposal execution-source handoff

The complete next-hop proposal compiler now verifies the original captured launch envelope, extracts only its original `NodeChainSource` fields, and supplies those fields to the existing strict execution-preparation constructor. It compares the resulting complete source digest and requested execution. It does not relax the generic parser, alter the retained suffix, start a native runtime, or accept arbitrary launch envelopes.

The reproduced original failure was in the three SS Server cases (3, 4 and 8 complete members). The nine typed SOCKS/HTTP/HTTPS Server cases passed. The caller had supplied a sealed launch envelope, including receipt fields, where the constructor requires the raw original source record. Local proposals skipped this constructor, while typed proposals used their distinct original source objects.

Validation uses the exact repaired source on `a86c99f18ec58cbeadbd195c0eb049f7a3c13013`; both touched preimages remain identical on `9d5307d0943e59eb24ef620bba3ef3a9a45af320`. All original 19 proposal/receipt roots (155 exact records) and one additional source-boundary root (13 records) passed in the actual complete client test package. This includes full Local/Server/Compare paths, source preservation, future profile Save/Load, unchanged original refusals and mailbox cleanup. The new source cases reject missing owners, changed envelopes/source fields and unsupported execution.

This is supplemental runtime evidence: the successful local build used `-vet=off` and no race instrumentation. The first ordinary functional build reached its unchanged 240-second cap before an image; the warm retry's vet subprocess was killed before tests. Both failures and unchanged source manifests are retained. Repository and shipping vet/race requirements are unchanged; this local pass does not establish those gates or installed/native encrypted acceptance. Test roots retain 120-second Go and 135-second process bounds, and all compilers retain 240 seconds.

Restoring only the old envelope call produced the required normal assertion failure in both Server and Compare 4-member SS cases: exact exit 1, named child/root/package failures, and no abort, skip or race markers. The mutant compiled in 100.468 seconds under the original 240-second bound. Exact current-head authoritative CI remains required; no full release-green claim is made.

## docs/DESKTOP-CANDIDATE-SAVED-POLICY-EVIDENCE-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop candidate prefix: saved imported protection

The desktop candidate preparation adapter now reads an imported member's
original device policy at its actual penultimate position. It handles the
already supported imported OpenVPN capture before attempting paired-node
capture. This mirrors the existing complete desktop chain policy constructor.
Previously the adapter recognized only imported Shadowsocks at position1;
an otherwise valid saved OpenVPN prefix preparation reached a nil paired node
and was refused during desktop protection capture.

The change is confined to
`internal/mobilemultihop/desktop_node_candidate_preparation_policy.go`. It does
not widen generic source parsing or candidate admission. All saved members
still contribute kill-switch, IPv6 and home-LAN restrictions; the final paired
profile remains the durable protection control. Only the selected WG/AWG
prefix contributes executable transports and physical roots. Application and
DNS ingress remain closed, and the saved OpenVPN/final suffix remains source
provenance rather than a runtime dependency.

## Focused validation

Source base: `638808faedacaf7b6482f0cf63027679c6e12675` plus this correction.
The new `desktop_node_candidate_preparation_imported_policy_test.go` exercises
the actual shared source, desktop physical projection and strict reopener:

- OpenVPN complete lengths3/4/8 on Linux, Windows and Darwin, rotating WG,
  AWG-fast/AWG-strong and TCP/UDP captures (nine leaves).
- The original imported Shadowsocks trio on all three desktop targets (three
  leaves); no unpublished longer imported-SS admission is assumed.
- Copied middle owner, changed middle policy, changed protection metadata,
  changed physical-root identity and foreign candidate catalog refusal (five
  leaves).

The fixture deliberately makes every paired member permissive and only the
uncontacted imported middle restrictive (`always`, IPv6Off, LAN denied). Thus
omitting that member cannot pass as a merely equivalent aggregate. Positive
cases also check final control identity, closed selector/ingress, absence of
suffix transports, original physical roots, detached exact replay, configured
MTU restore and over-ceiling refusal.

All20 unique Go run/pass records (17 leaves and three roots) passed with race
instrumentation in28.523 package seconds,81.463 seconds including compilation.
The original120-second Go bound and240-second wrapper bound were retained.
Source hashes were unchanged during execution. Positive log SHA-256:
`9c0fbc10d47c2421b878e9e606528e2c6ed6255e186446d6ac7ff8dc8b6c4c15`.

A separate race-instrumented replay restored the exact original638 production
helper through a read-only Go overlay, retaining the new test unchanged. The
Linux three-member OpenVPN case failed at its intended constructor witness,
`saved imported middle must retain desktop prefix protection`; no other test,
timeout or panic supplied the failure. The process joined in47.306 seconds.
Causal log SHA-256:
`fe18fce9d7adfb810fd12842cfea743096440d7823041c43767bb27184eb9987`.

The unit was guarded forward to
`b4558b31b11567fbf224c5714d17a2f5db5bc96e` after both runs joined. Its production
and new test bytes remained unchanged; the preceding execution evidence stays
attributed to638 rather than being reported as a rerun on the newer parent.

This proves source/projection/protection behavior. It does not claim an
installed Linux kernel, Windows WFP or Darwin PF/TUN execution, nor does it
add typed SOCKS/CONNECT candidate preparation or grant a saved suffix any
connection, DNS, Ready, Last or measurement authority.

## docs/DESKTOP-CONNECT-FINAL-LAYERS-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop CONNECT final Start Layers

The Windows, Linux and macOS ordered-path editors, saved connection profiles
and desktop CONNECT capture now preserve an independently selected Off,
AES-256-GCM or AES-256-GCM plus XOR whitening layer on the paired final node of
a Server/Compare HTTP or HTTPS CONNECT path. The imported CONNECT member
retains its original Off policy. The final's original private source, credentials,
typed shared compiler and exact UoT-v2 service remain required.

This change consumes the shared/native owner implementation published in
`e31d7d20d5862ecd86c3a3807ce752889d7138d8`. It removes the obsolete final-Off
consumer restrictions, while retaining canonical layer validation, complete
node references, transactional profile behavior, owned cancellation and the
already-proved Local/Server choice during Last restoration. Invalid XOR-only,
unknown and incorrectly typed selections remain rejected.

## Executed component evidence

| Check | Observed result | Scope |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Actual Go source, saved profiles, public controller and Last | Six roots / 31 race-instrumented records passed | All shipping production files compiled; focused original test-fixture closure described below |
| Compiled old-guard controls | Both controls rejected their exact intended faults | One restored the capture restriction; one restored the saved-profile restriction |
| Windows editor | 6,233 checks and two compiled controls passed | PowerShell 7.6.9 on Linux; explicit WPF doubles, `WPF=False` |
| macOS editor | 26,572 assertions and three compiled controls passed | Swift 6 on Linux; explicit AppKit/controller doubles |
| Linux editor model | Original model groups and all 27 compiled controls passed | Existing `--model-only` path, without a GTK display |
| Gate and validator contracts | Five CONNECT runner, eight macOS validator and twelve desktop editor validator tests passed | Integrated consumer composition |

The Go cases include independent entry/final layer combinations, four- and
eight-node profile round trips, HTTP and HTTPS Server execution, both Compare
winners and retained Last restoration. Public controller process/kernel replies
are explicit test doubles. The tests do not establish an installed desktop VPN,
real network performance, off-LAN interoperability or physical visual acceptance.

The source phase completed in 420.990 seconds, including a 198.631-second
compile. The two causal-control phases completed in 266.193 seconds, including
140.002- and 122.111-second compiles. Each compile retained the original
240-second bound; each selected test retained its 120-second Go timeout and
135-second process bound. The aggregate caller retained 1,200 seconds. The
controls require an ordinary test failure with the exact assertion witness;
a timeout, compiler failure, race report or signal cannot satisfy them. Source
snapshots remained unchanged throughout all successful executions.

## Local compiler and display limits

Two attempted full-package Go test compiles terminated with SIGKILL before any
test result. They are retained as failed attempts, not passes. The successful
local fallback kept every shipping production file selected by Go and 110
original test files containing the target cases, their conservative dependency
closure and existing test initializers. It overlaid only 454 unselected test
files with an empty package declaration. The causal controls additionally
overlaid their one original production guard. Every one of the 31 expected test
records remained mandatory. The complete-package native CI compile and gate
are unchanged and remain required for platform acceptance.

The full Linux GTK widget run could not start its owned Xvfb display because
the environment refused its listening sockets. Its full widget result remains
unproved locally. The existing model-only path passed; it is not reported as
GTK visual acceptance. The Windows process was started with the documented
PowerShell telemetry opt-out and update checks disabled after an initial
automatic-review block on telemetry. No network exception was added.

## Composition and required delivery checks

The desktop consumer was composed onto `3b4b5d71`, preserving the concurrent
direct OpenVPN source-fixture repair and the already-published Apple consumer.
All 21 consumer files retain the tested bytes. Two privately staged shared
prerequisite files were verified against their published versions and excluded
from this patch. The new desktop source and causal-control gates are registered
in the native builder and input inventory; they do not replace the original
CONNECT, editor, package or runtime checks.

This document records component evidence. Exact-current Windows/macOS/Linux
builds, package execution, publication, deployment, signing where applicable
and physical-device acceptance require their own results.

## docs/DESKTOP-CONNECTED-CANDIDATE-EVIDENCE.md

# Connected ordered-prefix candidate workflow

## Published source identities

| Unit | Published commit |
| --- | --- |
| Controller API, permanent freshness invalidation and save-time MTU hold | `edf69b3683d57ae930f5e5d99d5ae166ca19e1f0` |
| Native Windows controls | `f30990fd774f261177a9d1ca32c8bb88f19c951e` |
| Native AppKit controls and transport | `2a7101ad6a1f7248c49863ede1317e17fe703208` |
| Native GTK controls and mandatory widget fixture | `baec5478b5a3fff59cbf4560dbfc1c15db80492f` |

These identify published source units. They do not certify pending hosted
platform gates or a physical-device release.

## User-visible behavior

Performance on the native desktop clients has a separate **Candidates through
connected prefix** action. It captures an existing retained WG/AWG packet
prefix and original saved SS2022 sources. The user selects candidates and tests
50 TCP connections through that prefix. This measures destination TCP
reachability; it does not authenticate the candidate's credentials, measure
ICMP, or invent a private Router VPN identity for an imported source.

Only complete, drained, finite 50/50 receipts are scored. Results retain the
native producer's measurement timestamp and expire exactly 120 seconds later.
Status polling does not extend that timestamp. It observes the original saved
catalog, session, source files, native MTU reader and ready proof. Loss of any
original owner permanently invalidates that job's displayed/scored rows, even
if the previous values later reappear. A changed picker selection must be
captured before Test or Save becomes available.

**Save next-connection profile** is explicit and requires a profile name and
confirmation of the complete displayed path. A paired SS2022 candidate replaces
the suffix after the chosen packet prefix. A custom SS2022 candidate currently
supports the bounded three-member shape: original first WG/AWG node, the
original imported SS2022 source, and the original paired SS2022 final. Other
insertions remain unavailable. The actual ordered compiler validates the full
proposed original source before saving.

Saving appends a complete existing connection-profile record, preserving each
node's own policy and credential references. It does not rewrite the active
profile inventory, credentials, selected node, or native runtime. Normal Load
continues to require disconnecting before applying the saved profile.

## Controller protocol and ownership

- `GET /api/multihop/prefix-candidates/catalog?prefix_position=N` returns a
  bounded selection view with an opaque 256-bit token, exact eligible source
  identities, prefix choices and explicit proposed paths. It grants no path
  proof or measurement permission.
- `POST /api/multihop/prefix-candidates` requires the original view token,
  prefix position, distinct saved candidate IDs, a new 128-bit request ID and
  exactly 50 samples. The synchronous POST retains its original HTTP context;
  no detached background measurement job is created.
- `GET /api/multihop/prefix-candidates?request_id=...` observes that exact
  retained job. Its bounded observer runs outside the shared control lock. An
  atomic loss latch prevents stale results from reviving even if cancellation
  expires before the observer can reacquire the lock.
- `POST /api/multihop/prefix-candidates/cancel` reuses the existing candidate
  cancellation owner. Premature Cancel records the request nonce, so a delayed
  start cannot resurrect it.
- `POST /api/multihop/prefix-candidates/save-profile` requires the original
  request/view/candidate, a name and the exact proposed node list. It rechecks
  the live reader/proof, catalog and source compiler before atomic profile-store
  publication. Save reacquires the exact retained launch's MTU hold before
  rechecking the original reader and retains that hold through the atomic write.
  Any reader change while Auto was allowed to resume invalidates the receipt;
  the new reader is never substituted. A receipt permits one explicit save.

The existing operation lock, public Stop/shutdown admission and candidate
ownership are shared with the pre-connect pair workflow. Native exchange owns
its exact original source/pulse/request/cancel inodes. An unjoined failure keeps
its opaque retirement handle and original MTU hold; Cancel retries only that
handle. Replacement operations remain blocked until that owner joins. A late
successful response after user cancellation cannot re-enable a result in any
native dialog.

## Executable evidence boundaries

The Go HTTP fixture exercises the real connected owner/source capture, actual
native mailbox request, response adoption, joined MTU hold and complete saved
profile transaction. Its native response producer is an explicit boundary;
real encrypted 50-handshake evidence belongs to the independent mandatory
native prefix witness described in `ORDERED-PREFIX-CANDIDATE-NATIVE-EVIDENCE.md`.
The save fixture also checks that Retest/Auto cannot acquire the MTU mutex
during publication and that the hold is released afterward. Additional tests mutate the actual saved source, session and MTU mailbox, then
restore them, and require permanent row invalidation.

The Windows fixture executes the final launcher composition and actual dialog
functions/callbacks with explicit WPF/HTTP boundaries. The Swift fixture executes
extracted shipping controller methods and separately compiles/tests the real
bounded transport. AppKit object behavior still belongs to hosted macOS builds.
The GTK fixture compiles the shipping form with real GTK widgets under Xvfb and
controlled HTTP completion; it is mandatory in the existing native form lane.
No local GTK execution or physical/TUN acceptance is claimed where those
privileges or libraries are unavailable.

Local verification:

| Scope | Result and boundary |
| --- | --- |
| Complete API freshness suite | `-race` PASS, 217.456 seconds; original source/session/reader loss cannot revive rows |
| Final save-time MTU hold | Focused `-race` PASS, 129.130 seconds; both full validations and the original 20-second Save deadline retained |
| Windows composition | 38 existing Performance checks, 22 ordered-candidate checks and four existing causal controls passed under PowerShell 7.7 |
| Swift candidate controller and transport | 17 controller checks and 14 actual bounded transport checks passed |
| Swift live response integration | 71 existing checks and six causal controls passed |
| GTK | Mandatory shipping-form/real-widget/Xvfb fixture added; local execution unavailable, hosted result still required |

One prior focused Save attempt returned the opaque original-owner guard. The
same path subsequently passed with cancellation diagnostics added and all full
validations retained. Its exact earlier cause was not established; no deadline,
identity equality or source check was relaxed to obtain the passing result.
Packaged Windows PowerShell 5.1/WPF and actual AppKit runtime acceptance also
remain hosted evidence boundaries.

Commands:

```
go test -race -p=1 ./cmd/client -run '^TestOrderedCandidate' -count=1 -timeout=300s -v
pwsh -NoProfile -File client/test-windows-ordered-candidates.ps1 -Root .
python deploy/test_desktop_ordered_candidates.py --platform darwin
python deploy/test_desktop_ordered_candidates.py --platform linux
```

These units do not claim candidate authentication, arbitrary imported protocol
families, every ordered graph, or physical acceptance on every device. Existing
pre-connect pair measurement and connection-profile load guards remain in force.

## docs/DESKTOP-CONNECTED-TYPED-CANDIDATES-2026-11-16.md

# Connected candidate measurements through original desktop SOCKS and CONNECT paths

## Behavior and requirement scope

The desktop candidate chooser can measure a replacement node through an earlier
packet prefix of an already connected Local SOCKS5, HTTP CONNECT, or HTTPS CONNECT
chain. The measurement retains the complete original typed source and native
runtime. It performs fifty routed TCP connection samples and reports their robust
statistics; these samples are not ICMP ping, throughput, or HTTP response timings.

This extends the shared typed plans published in `fc203c75` into the actual desktop
controller and generated native command loop. It does not grant a closed saved
prefix connected-session authority. Server and Compare execution owners, mobile
host adoption, real encrypted packet traffic and installed-device acceptance are
separate requirements.

## Ownership and restoration

The controller admits an exclusive SOCKS or CONNECT source family and validates
the exact Ready record, active TUN reader, MTU, session and source catalog. The
native command loop constructs its plan from the original proved runtime and
retains its measurement activity until the worker and callback have drained.

The typed exchange has distinct request, source, live observation, result and
cancellation files. A nonce, monotonic generation, captured source hashes and
owned file identities bind those files to one operation. A copied source list or
an old result cannot grant authority. Cancellation and replacement preserve the
original pending owner until cleanup joins; they cannot remove a replacement
operation's files. Generic candidate mailboxes remain refused by typed owners.

The final receipt must match the exact capture, prefix, candidate and original
candidate source; include all fifty successful samples; retain its original
expiry; and pass current source, connection and reader validation. Saving the
measured proposal retains the full selected typed graph and its settings.

## Verification record

The controller baseline compiled the complete `cmd/client` package with Go
1.26.3, the race detector and default vet. All thirteen new roots (151 records)
passed, as did thirteen existing saved-prefix roots (80 records) on the recorded
`aa458312` plus draft source. Three separately compiled omissions failed at their
intended assertions: dropping typed admission, dropping capture identity, and
allowing a saved-prefix purpose to grant connected authority. Those controls used
the recorded `fc203c75` plus draft source. Test and process deadlines were not
increased.

Those controller fixtures explicitly model the child process and kernel. The
later `684a1c27` widened ordinary imported-Shadowsocks plan admission; the two
affected old fixture roots require their separate updated proof-refusal check.
The earlier 80-record result is not reported as a test of that newer assertion.

Native command-loop and Box baseline verification passed all seventeen groups:
85 executed records covering 78 unique test names. The nine positive SOCKS5,
HTTP CONNECT and HTTPS CONNECT cases each used three-, four- or eight-node
graphs and fifty actual destination TCP connections, for 450 connections. The
remaining groups exercised admission refusals, cancellation, joined cleanup,
original Box ownership and reader/MTU checks. The complete command and Box
packages compiled with Go 1.26.3, the race detector and default vet. Compilation
and all seventeen groups finished in 658.163 seconds inside the original
900-second aggregate; the 150-second Go test and 600-second process bounds
were retained.

That baseline used exact checkpoint 8 on `684a1c27`; it is not represented as a
run of the newer tree. The guarded forward to `47e4cd58` preserved the published
paired Shadowsocks AES/XOR target rules and new native peer-layer inputs. Only
the owned controller catalog and native build registration needed three-way
merges. Four focused source/runner suites then passed 5, 9, 4 and 4 tests on
checkpoint 9. An independent source review found no concrete blocker in the
captured source, runtime owner, readiness, reader, generation or drain paths.

All three current-source native omission controls passed their strict causal
verdicts on `47e4cd58` plus checkpoint 9. Removing the generic-mailbox refusal,
typed Ready reader or original drain guard failed at the required assertion.
Each control compiled the complete native command package, retained race and
default vet, and preserved the existing clocks. The three controls finished in
151.828 seconds total. Their verifier rejects compiler errors, timeouts, panics,
races, unexpected tests and unrelated assertion failures.

The native component cases exercise original runtime proofs, actual Box inbound
ownership and MTU readback, and actual loopback TCP/TLS destinations. Their
packet transport and kernel TUN are explicit component models. They do not
establish encrypted packet transit, an installed device or a complete release.

## Recorded source and validation identities

| Evidence | Source base | SHA-256 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Controller baseline receipt | `aa458312` plus recorded draft | `02923ff2a9ca831f46e50abad37666b746f75f1169b2900d33fc3f624c19a554` |
| Three controller omission controls | `fc203c75` plus recorded draft | `ae574d847a3471c27f2c552e10a51e52e9ef7d9b8080476b2e545f9bec03d513` |
| Seventeen-group native baseline | `684a1c27` plus checkpoint 8 | `f67004a3fb83bb2954cad4d4bf16e4efd099d84ebc49789984c7adc4e3b23028` |
| Checkpoint 8 source manifest | `684a1c27` | `fd38a58d6d93333a597ea2df114ec16c5c64d02805d92da575aee33276d766b9` |
| Checkpoint 9 source manifest | `47e4cd58` | `cf079202022f6442478a8f068691b4cf784e72e2b86e2438c1657ad2763e09b3` |
| Current focused source/runner suites | `47e4cd58` plus checkpoint 9 | `a7aefeec643da3885bd8934f1ef1de9faa8f1236b34be69fc0ced0dfac1d565c` |
| Three native omission controls | `47e4cd58` plus checkpoint 9 | `1fc0ee1c43f6d40db57ba61ec4aaafba6d36a6372d9a01905cba5dd21b8141b2` |
| Independent source review | `47e4cd58` plus checkpoint 9 | `22a087e85c302eda84a840f58d1fece01d2167af44d0316205e97f97a7b72769` |

All source snapshots, exact commands, output checksums, retained test-image

local subset does not establish an exact-SHA release or full product completion.

## docs/DESKTOP-HYSTERIA2-EXECUTION-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop paired Hysteria2 Server/Compare — November 16, 2026

This source unit admits wholly paired routes with 2–8 members: WG/AWG prefixes followed by a Hysteria2 final with Start Layer Off. Server and Compare use the existing generic schema1 preparation and physical owner. Imported-middle routes retain their separate final-Shadowsocks restrictions. Local behavior remains available.

The parent retains the original captured profiles, identity inventory, Hysteria authentication/TLS/obfuscation source, selected DNS, protection policy and MTU through preparation, selected Ready proof, public status and immutable proved Last restoration. Windows, GTK and AppKit use a distinct bounded capability and revalidate source tokens and final-layer constraints. Darwin admits only this paired family through its existing child/PF retirement path; unrelated paired families do not inherit its capability.

## Local evidence

The full canonical Linux parent race/vet compilation passed in 101.229 seconds under the unchanged 240-second limit. Three source/profile roots produced 36 complete records. These exercise actual generic preparations for Linux, Windows and Darwin targets at 2/4/8 members, saved profile Save/Load/Update, original physical binding and explicit source refusals.

The same immutable image passed 12 public/restoration roots with 25 records: the original proxy-admission regression, nine 2/4/8 Server/Compare choices, and 2/8-member Last restoration for both saved winners. These execute actual public handlers/controllers and original proof/MTU/status/Stop checks using the existing explicit native-child and kernel boundary models. Production four-second observation limits and 120-second test/135-second process limits are unchanged. They do not demonstrate installed provider traffic.

The initial source compilation failed on a missing test import, which was corrected before the successful image. A first control compiler was killed by a signal before any assertion ran; its cause was not established. The retry uses the same actual full source, race mode and limits with a 900 MiB Go memory target. All three retry compilers passed in 118.740, 117.772 and 123.962 seconds. Each mutant then failed only its intended assertion: removed final-Off guard, removed unique identity guard, and substituted retained physical owner. Each has its own image and stdout hash; a compiler crash is not counted as a causal pass.

The actual PowerShell editor passed its full 10,043-check callback baseline and three selected original controls in 114.750 seconds. Startup telemetry opt-out, NoProfile and inherited IPv4/IPv6 socket denial were in force. This is value/widget-model callback evidence, not WPF rendering. An initial scratch launcher failed before PowerShell because it named an absent Python executable; that preflight failure was retained.

The actual Swift source program passed 34,943 callbacks and three compiled controls in 73.513 seconds. The controls cover the new distinct capability, the original final-layer guard and same-ID relinking. Its first run exposed a new fixture bug: changing a backing selection did not select the visible execution menu. Only the test setup was corrected to exercise that actual menu. Production code was unchanged. Linux Swift execution does not establish AppKit rendering.

The GTK C model reached a final cumulative 5,616 checks and passed three compiled controls in 6.653 seconds. Real display callbacks, including the new Hysteria Server/Compare requests and missing-capability refusal, remain required by the full hosted gate; no local display execution is claimed.

The entire Darwin ARM64 parent test package compiled and passed vet in 154.491 seconds under its original 240-second bound. This cross-compilation is without race or execution. The newly required Darwin tests exercise original public admission/preparation dispatch and PF/child retirement ownership; successful installed Darwin traffic remains a separate acceptance boundary.

The 27 runner/inventory roots passed. Eight focused source-audit roots include the unchanged positive aggregate and actual mutations for every altered admission predicate. All original hosted editor controls remain mandatory; the focused local runs do not claim all 29/36/79 controls executed locally.

## Source and composition identity

The canonical Linux source/profile run, retained-image public/Last run, three compiled controls and Darwin cross-compilation used source digest `d73a0f33f664f28395c8e835e001d8aa0eef1c222f20bcd63412b506b5d22a07`. The retained Linux image SHA256 is `091fa940efc4c047156714f013a5467229fe0eaf50bc81d45b37943bac59c6e7`. The 36 source records and 25 public/restoration records are separate inventories; reruns and causal failures are not added as new positive cases.

Final composition is based on `1e2eb94ccb8a49688ab8a5f09909f8d3dae6f298`. Guarded forwarding preserved the newer OpenVPN-only fresh-observation implementation and its audit branches, the native Hysteria gate registrations, Android/Apple changes and saved-prefix work. Only the two audit files and central builder required merges among this unit's existing changed files; its production desktop/UI and Go test bytes remain unchanged. Historical compiled evidence remains attached to its actual snapshot; final composed source audits are recorded separately. Full current-head canonical/native execution remains required CI evidence.

## Required hosted acceptance

The new mandatory parent gate requires complete source inventories, actual public/Last roots on Linux and Windows, Darwin source/admission/PF-retirement roots, and strict compiled omission witnesses. Existing editor gates retain all original controls, with one new Hysteria capability control each (29 Windows, 36 GTK, 79 Swift).

The separately published native Hysteria Server/provider prerequisite is commit `7d2e2711269ed0fd13e1f6a95b7391646426789c`, documented in [HYSTERIA2-SERVER-NATIVE-EVIDENCE-2026-11-16.md](HYSTERIA2-SERVER-NATIVE-EVIDENCE-2026-11-16.md). Its encrypted 22-case gate remains mandatory on both Linux architectures; local namespace acceptance was unavailable. Parent/controller models, source compilation and portable editor callbacks do not establish privileged namespaces, installed PF/TUN, physical devices, package signing, deployed servers or a complete release. Native Darwin successful traffic and physical UI rendering remain hosted/platform evidence.

## docs/DESKTOP-HYSTERIA2-FINAL-LAYERS-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop Hysteria2 final Start Layers

Date: 2026-11-16. This is a bounded completion of the requested independent
Start Layer choice for a paired Hysteria2 final. The full product requirement
ledger, installed-device acceptance and exact published release remain separate.

## Behavior and ownership

The actual Windows, GTK and AppKit editors admit Off, AES-256-GCM and
AES-256-GCM plus XOR whitening for a paired Hysteria2 final after the original
WG/AWG prefixes. The actual desktop source and execution compiler retains the
complete two-to-eight-node selection for Local, Server and Compare. Invalid
layer strings or types, duplicate identities, imported-middle restrictions and
changed original credentials remain rejected. An editor choice does not grant
runtime proof by itself.

Saved profiles retain the full selected source inventory and final layer.
Startup/Last receipts explicitly serialize a non-Off Hysteria2 final layer, and
validate the exact original WG identity, Hysteria2 source and SS layer source in
order. The canonical Off receipt omits the new optional field and retains its
previous serialization. A source digest alone cannot authorize a new final
layer, and a different valid layer cannot borrow the original captured owner.

The final layer remains independent of each preceding node's layer. Hysteria2
credentials, Salamander, TLS server name and private trust remain the captured
values. Existing public-connect, Compare, physical-policy, cancellation,
retirement and restoration paths are exercised with their explicit native-child
and kernel-observation doubles; encrypted transport acceptance belongs to the
separate real-provider gate.

## Native editor/model evidence

| Executed source | Positive assertions | Focused compiled/mutated controls | Scope |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| Actual PowerShell editor functions | 10,049 | 3 | Source/serialization without WPF rendering |
| Actual GTK C editor model | 5,679 | 3 | Model and persistence; no display/widget callback acceptance |
| Actual Swift editor/callback source | 35,257 | 3 | Shipping source in the standalone native harness; no installed AppKit/device acceptance |

PowerShell completed in 65.749 seconds under its original 90-second limit. Its
three controls retained 45 seconds each. GTK retained 90-second compilation and
45-second runtime limits. Swift compiled in 12.667 seconds and ran in 9.390
seconds under separate original 90-second limits. Only the three listed Swift
controls were actually executed locally; the complete registered inventory
remains mandatory in the hosted source-validation gate.

The UI source hashes were rechecked unchanged after composing the earlier
shared/Apple and complete desktop-candidate Save changes. Receipts and each
compiled control bind the exact source, image, log, return code and deadline.
The earlier PowerShell observer expected the wrong failure-label prefix; its
actual unchanged child log was reparsed with the correct strict label. An early
Swift old-Off control threw at top level, so it was not accepted as causal
proof. Its test positive was changed to report an ordinary assertion failure,
and the positive plus all three controls were rerun. Neither failed observer or
crash is counted as a successful control.

The controller runner, profile-mode runner and strict macOS-source runner passed
5, 16 and 8 checks respectively. The existing backend-session and Speed Lab
source audits also passed. These audits are not native-network evidence.

## Controller and Last evidence

The actual full controller package was compiled with Go 1.26.3, `-race`,
`-p=1` and `-mod=readonly`, using the existing 240-second compiler bound and
task-local default resources (two CPUs, GOGC 50, GOMEMLIMIT 1800 MiB). The first
image completed in 101.774 seconds. It produced these strictly parsed results:

| Complete test roots executed | Pass records | Meaning |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| Six source/admission/profile roots | 91 | Exact canonical layers, full original sources, source changes and saved profiles |
| All 19 public connection/restore roots | 52 | Server/Compare choices and restoration for Off, AES and AES plus XOR |
| Nine existing Local and Startup/Last roots | 72 | Prior source, receipt, policy and legacy serialization behavior |

The first image was built at private composed commit
`bef941c114ea0441ade04c989bcb28ed9436c973` on published `b266aab7`.
Its complete input digest is
`9cf6a5ebc63e4bb2e22d8dfd39e752c8aa4db44da8a53fae1b3afb56b00f4c1d`;
its race image SHA-256 is
`f3ef98488f326b90e8516d68ff735fb01e4a631ae59a60e20c6d85aca6cbcab7`.
The public and regression receipt digests are respectively
`fe5ac375202ffe17374c77f4ba6e384aa4c282547f9c84d52ab8dcc508b4a527` and
`3cd1b7d9a12aa9ca6b5edf36b749301f6530f5d3bc0c6ab69d9962abc5adfff1`.

The new Startup root initially failed before reaching the capture because its
fixture lacked the explicit native-package boundary. Its unused environment
variable was replaced with the same private package-presence manifest used by
the existing Startup source fixtures. This changes only test setup: it neither
installs a runtime nor relaxes the production platform check. The failure log
is retained and is not included in the pass counts above.

After preserving the later typed saved-prefix host work through published
`9d5307d0943e59eb24ef620bba3ef3a9a45af320`, the corrected complete package
compiled again with race instrumentation in 88.634 seconds under the same
240-second bound. Its private composed commit was
`6b3f7f5e7de18200acb4532671f64532a6bc31cb`, input digest
`1357a8513ce1738a9795dfa323cbf8bd81399bf31ff04306a8d40b8df41905f0`, and
image SHA-256
`dc56d58539f4576730198a6cf8461716a5635e48ada74d9b589bf039125b77d1`.
The complete corrected Startup root then passed all five root/leaf records in
0.825 seconds. The earlier 215 passing records belong to the first image;
only this corrected root was rerun on the later composed image. The untouched
Hysteria controller/editor source and assertion bodies were preserved during
composition; these local results are not described as a full exact-head CI run.

The local malformed-layer mutation did not reach a test verdict. Both full
race compilations of that control were terminated with `compile: signal:
killed`, after 61.034 seconds at the default resources and 98.136 seconds at
two CPUs/GOGC 20/1200 MiB. Both retained the original 240-second limit; neither
produced a control executable. The cause of the signal is not established by
the available process namespace or cgroup telemetry. The second control was
not launched locally after those failures. The failed logs are retained and
neither compilation is counted as a passing negative control. All four
compiled controller controls remain mandatory in the hosted gate.



The mandatory hosted inventory still includes all seven source roots, all 19
public roots on the applicable hosts, the Darwin-specific source/ownership
roots, and all four controller mutation controls. The changed malformed-layer control was attempted locally as recorded above;
the new old-Off, duplicate-identity and physical-owner controls were not rerun
locally and remain mandatory in CI.
The controller tests use explicit native-child and kernel-observation doubles.
They prove controller behavior, not a tunnel on an installed user device.


## Remaining acceptance

The required Windows/macOS/Linux native builds and exact-source packaged gates
must execute on the published commit. The separate Hysteria2 encrypted gate passed all 36 complete Server cases on
each Linux architecture at published
`b266aab790b68436f2ebaaacf67d25c2b6103d33`: [AMD64 job](https://api.github.com/repos/Eabusham2/router-vpn/actions/jobs/133308487486)
and [ARM64 job](https://api.github.com/repos/Eabusham2/router-vpn/actions/jobs/133308486903).
Both exact raw job logs were independently reparsed with the current strict
36-case validator. This evidence belongs to that native change, rather than
the later desktop consumer commit. A successful
editor or controller fixture does not establish installed-device networking,
leak/network-transition behavior, signing, private deployment, or a green final
release.

## docs/DESKTOP-HYSTERIA2-PHYSICAL-FACTORY-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop Hysteria2 physical factory and Start Layers

Date: 2026-11-16. This closes the physical-factory rejection left after the
desktop Hysteria2 editor/controller work. It is a bounded source and native
package result, with exact-release and installed-device acceptance separate.

## Shipping change

`routerVPNDesktopNodeChainPhysicalTarget` now admits the already validated
paired Hysteria2 final on Linux, Windows and Darwin with its captured Off,
AES-256-GCM or AES-256-GCM plus XOR Start Layer. The original complete policy,
platform, per-prefix proof, original Box configuration, and physical ownership
checks still precede preparation and activation. Imported-middle and typed
OpenVPN/SS restrictions are unchanged. The first paired WG/AWG node remains the
only physical root; a final Start Layer grants no additional direct egress.

The real Box fixtures cover WG, AWG2 Fast and AWG2 Strong prefixes, both encrypted
final layers, each captured desktop target, and Server/Compare preparation from
the original source. A changed outer credential, inner detour, copied policy or
foreign target cannot borrow the original factory. Darwin's changed-Box refusal
calls its Darwin factory explicitly. The existing platform factory tests remain.

## Executed native evidence

Canonical preparation used the pinned core
`1ac1a339cb1223e9c70eae14c44411c75033c02d` and Xray
`50231eaff98ccc31b5cbd247a721c16e97fe5ec1`, including the actual whitening
registration. The complete prepared inventory has 2691 records with SHA-256
`a6547eb49a517157892a65497f6356fe5626f34dc7f0c8ed4f18bcb148822343`.
The composed source was private commit `1dc62c2021a9166b806667ef77e8b625c7e6ea69`
on published `62665c33`; the later `48a05814` adds an unrelated source-inventory
check and changes none of these compiled production or fixture bytes.

Go 1.26.3 compiled the actual native package with `-race -p=1 -mod=readonly`
and its shipping WireGuard, gVisor, QUIC, uTLS, OpenVPN, Naive and pure-Go tags.
The positive compilation took 205.438 seconds within its original
600-second compiler deadline. All **84 exact root/leaf records** passed in
33.153 seconds with the original 90-second Go timeout and 105-second
outer deadline. Parsing rejects missing, duplicate, skipped, foreign, failed,
panicking, timed-out or race-contaminated records.

The positive race image is `8461f6e419ccf24faade7494f1cb889229fb792a64968178969bb7ecf6a21423`.
Its complete run log digest is `e94f1910f9468371188aacb8cbbd296c41255020b6f46e211455d728065227f0`.
Positive receipt: `0ac0cb86b6ab9bcd4b0068ef888c4ecf66a3c0cae362eb3221bdb38e11c70688`.

Each control below compiled an actual changed shipping source through a Go
overlay, used the original deadlines, ran only its registered root/leaf and
failed with the exact intended assertion. The complete source inventory was
rechecked unchanged before and after each execution.

| Compiled control | Compile seconds | Run seconds | Observed outcome |
| --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| ordered-final-legacy-factory | 64.264 | 0.466 | Intended assertion refusal |
| ordered-final-foreign-platform | 49.910 | 0.417 | Intended assertion refusal |
| ordered-final-off-only | 44.650 | 0.366 | Intended assertion refusal |

Control receipt: `497d05167f0aee420869102b53e9589981ebd05f193559b055fbeea433e43888`.
The Off-only control reinstates the actual old final-layer rejection; the
foreign-platform control removes only the captured-target comparison. The
legacy factory control continues to detect rejection of every Hysteria2 final.

## Required native gates

The ordinary physical inventory expands from 52 to 84 records. The Darwin Box
phase expands from 11 to 64 records by including the complete 53-record Hysteria2
physical inventory; its total native inventory is 395 and its compiled control
inventory is 47. The original positive and negative records remain required.
The focused physical and Darwin runner checks passed 20 checks. Their strict
parent/leaf accounting remains unchanged; new table cases use explicit leaf
names so no nonexistent intermediate subtest is accepted.

## Boundaries

The local execution used Linux and the actual Box package with the existing
explicit physical-service fixtures. It checks captured target and native graph
ownership, and does not claim a real Windows or macOS kernel tunnel, a running
private Hysteria2 server, installed-device transitions or leak acceptance.
Native hosted platform jobs and the complete exact-commit release remain
required. Earlier disk-full compiler attempts produced no test image or verdict
and are not included above; their original deadlines and failed logs were kept.
The final successful preparation came from fresh pinned sources, not from an
in-place re-preparation of an already patched native core.

## docs/DESKTOP-IMPORTED-LOCAL-EDITORS-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop Local imported-hop profile capture

The Windows, Linux and macOS ordered editors now preserve each independently
imported Shadowsocks hop at its original interior position in supported Local
paths of three through eight members. Paired WireGuard/AmneziaWG members retain
their original positions and the final remains a paired Shadowsocks exit. Each
external member must expose the exact Boolean
`ordered_local_imported_nodes_supported` capability from its captured catalog.

The editors preserve the full node/mode ordering and source-token inventory
through capture, Save/Load and Connect. They send private-node references, not
copied passwords or bundled protocol assets. Relinked members, duplicate IDs,
missing or non-Boolean capabilities, substituted protocols, an imported entry
or final, and an incompatible final are rejected. This Local addition does not
borrow Server/Compare authority or widen the existing SOCKS5, CONNECT or OpenVPN
position contracts. The desktop controller and ordinary latency readers were
published as separate prerequisite units.

## Executed evidence

| Platform | Actual local verification | Bound and result |
|---|---|---|
| Windows | Shipping PowerShell ordered-editor callbacks with explicit value-only widget boundaries: 10,550 checks. Two new independently executed causal controls restore the old penultimate-only position rule or remove the strict source-capability guard. | Baseline 80.306 seconds; controls 3.640 and 2.602 seconds. Each retained its 90-second bound. Baseline exited zero; both controls exited one on their intended assertion. |
| Linux | Compiled shipping C/json-glib source model, serialization and persistence. The cumulative shipping counter ends at 6,327; it is not the sum of its three progress counters. All 35 compiled model controls rejected their intended defects. | 55.870 seconds under the existing 120-second aggregate bound. Source fingerprints were unchanged. GTK display callbacks were explicitly outside this model-only invocation. |
| macOS | Actual extracted shipping AppKit profile/callback logic compiled with Foundation and explicit widget, HTTP and scheduler doubles. The shipping baseline passed 36,202 checks. The first 80 of 83 compiled controls passed, including both new Local imported-position and capability controls. | The aggregate attempt reached its 900-second outer bound and was terminated and joined. The original 90-second compilation/runtime child bounds were not changed. This is a timed-out aggregate run, not a complete gate PASS. |

The three uncompleted macOS controls are `hysteria-execution-capability`,
`hysteria-final-layer-required`, and `paired-ss-execution-capability`. They are
existing controls after the successfully executed Local additions. Their
remaining verification and the hosted complete native workflow stay required.
No control was removed or its assertion weakened to publish this change.

The macOS execution source is
`241a6da52781865e775b52bc6792c538112d8a71`. All 33 recorded macOS inputs match the
composed publication byte-for-byte. The earlier Windows and Linux receipts
record file fingerprints rather than a Git source ID; their two and 62 recorded
inputs, respectively, also match this composition. Those older receipts retain
their original identity and are not presented as newly rerun tests.

| Retained output | SHA-256 |
|---|---|
| Windows shipping callback output | `416e06ce99e7c5ed1e4187e9bc70e8d8b8f3301dca3093bf357374568c304c60` |
| Windows imported-position control | `6e5310b2de079080dc884f7fc56e7aef51badb7c1912357994766c0864126fd7` |
| Windows capability control | `aa64efcb813019b5dc620bd2365615192c73df7ae2c9444e854289177c3eec9e` |
| Linux complete model output | `b922d0238a3d2b86570118b8e9772ef6af1df03af994c44a85af39aa17b80fa1` |
| macOS timed-out aggregate output | `e01374dd96c791e6ac5dee29970a286690616523d915130205b248b9f2c579ed` |

Local receipts are retained under
`multiple-imported-desktop-validation/corrected-controls`,
`multiple-imported-desktop-validation/gtk-model-composed`, and
`multiple-imported-desktop-validation/macos-241a` beneath the recorded
`/workspace/scratch/00a34583247b` workspace. The publication also checks that the
Windows shipping control inventory and its required runner agree exactly.

## Completed macOS residual verification

A bounded continuation on the same unchanged
`241a6da52781865e775b52bc6792c538112d8a71` inputs compiled and executed the exact
three uncompleted controls plus a fresh shipping baseline. The baseline again
passed 36,202 checks; `hysteria-execution-capability`,
`hysteria-final-layer-required`, and `paired-ss-execution-capability` each
rejected their intended behavioral defect. The continuation completed in
57.645 seconds under its 300-second aggregate bound, retaining every original
90-second compilation/runtime child deadline. All processes joined and all
33 recorded source inputs remained unchanged.

The continuation extracts the original matrix construction and witnesses from
the unchanged shipping Python gate. It asserts that the first attempt's 80
control names are the exact ordered prefix and these three names are the entire
remaining suffix. It does not edit any generated Swift assertion, substitute
an expected outcome, or rerun only a fabricated smaller model. Together the
original and continued attempts cover all 83 original controls; this is not a
claim that the original timed-out invocation passed or that one complete
aggregate run succeeded.

Continuation output SHA-256:
`1ab2cedd90267fa95726b6e06d131e92907f97278b4244b80649db57e56b0aae`.
The retained `macos-241a-residual/summary.json` records exact generated program
and executable hashes, individual durations, witnesses, and the complete
83-control coverage inventory. Hosted native compilation and installed-device
acceptance remain separate.

These callback and model results do not establish real WPF, GTK or AppKit visual
acceptance, packaged tunnel behavior, device networking, deployment or a green
exact-SHA release. Those remain separate gates. Mobile consumers and schema6
Server/Compare integration are separate implementation units.

## docs/DESKTOP-OPENVPN-EDITOR-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop OpenVPN ordered editor — November 16, 2026

The Windows, macOS and Linux ordered editors now retain a separately paired
OpenVPN intermediate when selecting Server or Compare. This continues the
original ordered custom-node, complete connection-profile and execution-choice
requirements. It connects the public editor contract to the distinct native
child published in `d4fc8d41`; public controller adoption is a separate unit.

## Editor contract

A path contains three through eight nodes: one through six independently
linked WireGuard or AmneziaWG prefixes, the original OpenVPN intermediate,
and a paired Shadowsocks 2022 final. The OpenVPN intermediate retains Start
Layer Off. Every paired prefix and the final retain their own canonical Off,
AES-256-GCM or AES-256-GCM plus XOR whitening choice.

Server and Compare require the exact boolean public capability
`ordered_openvpn_execution_middle_supported` for that imported source and
the controller's distinct `ordered_openvpn_executions` capability. Generic,
Shadowsocks, CONNECT or SOCKS capabilities cannot authorize this path.
The controller remains responsible for validating the original literal
OpenVPN transport and independent Local/Server provider pairing. Missing or
nonboolean hints fail closed; a display hint cannot create a native owner.

Editor requests contain the original ordered references, selected execution,
selected final identity and source-token inventory. They do not copy private
provider credentials. A relinked or replaced member cannot inherit an older
draft. Complete profile save, load and update retain the selected path and
execution without implicitly connecting. Existing Local and other protocol
families retain their original capability checks.

## Executed evidence

| Platform source | Local execution | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Windows | Actual shipping PowerShell editor functions and extracted callbacks; widget/controller boundaries doubled on Linux | 10,013 assertions passed; three deliberate capability/layer changes were rejected with their intended assertions |
| macOS | Actual shipping Swift profile, editor, deferred-controller and Settings callbacks; AppKit/controller boundaries doubled | 34,787 assertions passed; three separately compiled capability/boolean/layer controls were rejected |
| Linux | Actual compiled C, JSON and on-disk GKeyFile model; no display | Positive source model passed; all 30 compiled controls were rejected, including the three new OpenVPN controls |

The cases cover all six supported path lengths, WG/AWG bases, independent
paired layers, both requested execution choices, exact source tokens, private
credential exclusion, incompatible final modes, malformed layer values,
missing or substituted capabilities, and relinked members. The AppKit and
GTK fixture programs also include the full-profile callbacks for the new
family; GTK widget callbacks remain for the hosted display gate.

All 16 runner tests passed, retaining the exact Windows control inventory and
strict causal verdict rules. Each Windows or Swift child kept the original
90-second bound. The Windows positive run took 75.336 seconds; its controls
took 47.317–51.090 seconds. The four Swift compile/run pairs completed in
76.040 seconds total. The GTK model and controls completed in 62.629 seconds,
with unchanged 90-second compilation and 45-second execution limits.

The first Windows and GTK attempts correctly refused a new test fixture whose
final transport literal had been altered while expanding the matrix. The
fixture was corrected to its original Shadowsocks final; no production
validation was relaxed. The Windows source retains its original UTF-8 BOM
for PowerShell 5.1. Both failed attempts and the corrected receipts remain
recorded. The Swift source and generated fixture were unchanged by that
correction, and its successful source-bound result is retained.

Validation log SHA-256 identities:

- Windows final: `2d22f1db3e5a6984274066b83bd60351b1847b08a5e40e234d97847089da47aa`.
- macOS: `d331bf015305e44aeec7be7969bf9c335d7a088dbf083e79078ab5e34f4ae75c`.
- GTK final: `cef524db3a44a4bc5a71b1a9b0aebd0371b403570191676826290c081fe96027`.

## Remaining evidence

These checks establish editor and profile behavior against explicit controller
boundaries. They do not establish a system VPN or native encrypted traffic.
The matching desktop public controller/owner integration, hosted WPF/AppKit/GTK
execution, exact-SHA packages and release, device networking and private
deployment retain their separate acceptance requirements. A controller that
has not published the OpenVPN capability continues to refuse this execution.

## Required wrapper inventories

The editor addition exposed three stale wrapper inventories at `7ea16c36`.
The strict macOS wrapper still required 75 controls instead of the 78 now
prepared by the shipping suite; Linux expected 32 instead of 35, and Windows
expected 25 instead of 28. The wrappers now require the exact published
OpenVPN capability and final-layer controls in their actual order. Every
previous control and the missing, duplicate, substituted, failed and unjoined
result refusals remain required.

All 21 Python wrapper tests passed on the integrated source. They check the
actual 79 prepared Swift programs without executing Swift, and the exact
Windows witness and GTK control inventories. Additional OpenVPN witness
omission, duplication and substitution checks fail as intended. This is
source and verdict-runner evidence; it is not another native UI execution.
The Windows child bound remains 90 seconds; the existing derived whole-suite
budget now accounts for the three added commands: `(22 + 1 + 28) * 90 = 4590`
seconds. The GTK 900-second and macOS 1800-second bounds are unchanged.

## docs/DESKTOP-OPENVPN-EXECUTION-RECORDS-2026-11-16.md

# Desktop OpenVPN execution records — November 16, 2026

This prerequisite supplies the private adoption and readiness records used by
the desktop OpenVPN Server/Compare integration. It continues the captured
session and Local/Server requirements in the reconciled ledger. It does not
by itself publish a desktop host integration or prove a connected device.

## Record ownership

The adoption parser reconstructs the schema-five execution policy from the
parent's original preparation. The child record carries its typed Server
descriptor and request, transfer identity, projected engine/network/session
identity, and captured physical-source digest. It does not repeat the Local
source or replace the parent's captured private settings.

The readiness parser requires canonical bounded JSON, the same preparation
and transfer, selected epoch, proof generation, reader, interface and MTU,
the selected DNS capture, and complete per-member proof results. It preserves
the required average of the last-node and external response times. A failed
candidate must have a failure reason and no score. The desktop parent must
also retain the actual child and independently check freshness and native
interface readback; a record alone grants no process, route, TUN or lease.

The imported OpenVPN member's completed HTTPS proof now explicitly reports
`public-path` and the existing cumulative-path label. Private Router proof
rows retain their existing presentation. This prevents a public provider
measurement from being presented as a private Router-agent response.

## Completed validation

The frozen source was based on
`492d0d58b2965a7d172ab13844b8532dff5cc1ff`. Its six changed paths had unchanged
preimages at the later integration base
`1365b7ac3fa57d7c5666a097b9ee88e8dcb4ef7c`, preserving the newer Apple and
desktop CONNECT consumer work.

Four actual race-instrumented test roots passed **118 test records**:

| Root | Passed records | Coverage |
| --- | ---: | --- |
| `TestDesktopOpenVPNExecutionCanonicalRecords` | 103 | Linux, Windows and Darwin record formats; Local and Server selection; foreign identity, transfer, DNS, epoch, proof, score, source, family, MTU and presentation refusals |
| `TestDesktopOpenVPNExecutionMaximumRecords` | 13 | Complete eight-node records, original reconstruction, foreign family and source/key refusals |
| `TestNodeChainOpenVPNExecutionRuntimeServerOwnsOriginalLease` | 1 | Actual shared Server proof completion and public-row presentation |
| `TestNodeChainOpenVPNExecutionCompareKeepsSharedTargetAndFreshWinner` | 1 | Actual shared Compare proof completion and public-row presentation |

The test process and Go test limits remained 240 and 180 seconds respectively.
The complete checked source stayed unchanged. The successful execution receipt
has SHA-256
`5a5c67368227f60646f67f9eeeb049ef81c9d172798771dfae1cef7ab2ef258d`;
the frozen six-file manifest has SHA-256
`b6fa577f1a6bb9b5510da282ce3941fb25f78cac0adee4851ae2f51455b3b2b1`.
An earlier compiler termination and an unavailable optional timing executable
were retained as separate unsuccessful attempts, with no test-pass claim.

The shared runtime fixtures have explicit native-I/O boundaries. Actual native
child/Box execution, desktop UI callbacks, installed-device networking, signed
distribution and exact-release publication remain separate acceptance stages.
